{"etymology_number":"3","forms":[{"form":"flushes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A sudden flowing; a rush which fills or overflows, as of water for cleansing purposes."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Particularly, such a cleansing of a toilet."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The process of clearing the contents of a buffer or cache."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A suffusion of the face with blood, as from fear, shame, modesty, or intensity of feeling of any kind; a blush; a glow."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the flush on the side of a peach; the flush on the clouds at sunset","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any tinge of red color like that produced on the cheeks by a sudden rush of blood."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"a flush of joy","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A sudden flood or rush of feeling; a thrill of excitement, animation, etc."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A line of poles or obstacles that a skier must weave between."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","skiing","sports"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈflʌʃ/"}],"upstream_line":297156,"upstream_offset":1484778909,"upstream_record_sha256":"a0cf2914e8f120206ca873b8b755ef1191f806307336e9cf2dafab7c82af1da1","word":"flush"}
